<p>Specs or specifications are structured artifacts that formalize the development process for features and bug fixes in your application. They provide a systematic approach to transform high-level ideas into detailed implementation plans with clear tracking and accountability.</p>
<div class="my-8 overflow-x-auto [&amp;&gt;table]:my-0"><table><thead><tr><th>Capability</th><th style="text-align:center">IDE</th><th style="text-align:center">CLI</th><th style="text-align:center">Web</th><th style="text-align:center">Mobile</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Feature Specs</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td></tr><tr><td>Bugfix Specs</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td></tr><tr><td>Quick Spec</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td></tr><tr><td>Parallel task execution</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td></tr><tr><td><a class="" href="/docs/specs/analyze-requirements/">Analyze Requirements</a></td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td></tr><tr><td><a class="" href="/docs/specs/correctness/">Correctness</a> (property-based testing)</td><td style="text-align:center">✓</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td><td style="text-align:center">—</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>With Kiro&#x27;s specs, you can:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Break down requirements</strong> into user stories with acceptance criteria</li>
<li><strong>Build design docs</strong> with sequence diagrams and architecture plans</li>
<li><strong>Track implementation progress</strong> across discrete tasks</li>
<li><strong>Collaborate effectively</strong> between product and engineering teams</li>
</ul>
<div class="heading-anchor-wrapper"><h2 id="core-structure">Core Structure</h2><a href="#core-structure" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Copy link to Core structure section"><span class="anchor-icon-wrapper"><svg class="anchor-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 17" width="16" height="17" fill="currentColor"><path d="M2.27143 14.228C0.591897 12.5485 0.591897 9.82539 2.27143 8.14585L3.42324 6.99404L3.44188 6.97635C3.63816 6.799 3.94118 6.80488 4.13035 6.99404C4.31951 7.18321 4.32539 7.48623 4.14804 7.6825L4.13035 7.70115L2.97854 8.85296C1.68953 10.142 1.68953 12.2319 2.97854 13.5209L3.00888 13.5509C4.30014 14.8098 6.36751 14.7998 7.64646 13.5209L8.79827 12.3691L8.81691 12.3514C9.01319 12.174 9.31621 12.1799 9.50538 12.3691C9.69454 12.5582 9.70046 12.8613 9.52311 13.0576L9.50538 13.0762L8.35356 14.228C6.68715 15.8944 3.99347 15.9074 2.311 14.267L2.27143 14.228ZM13.0215 3.47793C11.7425 2.19899 9.67517 2.18896 8.3839 3.44794L8.35356 3.47793L7.22579 4.6057C7.03053 4.80096 6.71395 4.80096 6.51869 4.6057C6.32342 4.41044 6.32342 4.09386 6.51869 3.89859L7.64646 2.77082L7.68603 2.73177C9.3685 1.09138 12.0622 1.10441 13.7286 2.77082L13.7677 2.81036C15.3952 4.47958 15.3951 7.14417 13.7677 8.81338L13.7286 8.85296L12.6008 9.98073C12.4056 10.176 12.089 10.176 11.8937 9.98073C11.6985 9.78547 11.6985 9.46888 11.8937 9.27362L13.0215 8.14585L13.0515 8.11551C14.3005 6.83442 14.3005 4.78937 13.0515 3.50827L13.0215 3.47793Z"></path><path d="M10.0219 5.86632L10.0406 5.84858C10.2369 5.67124 10.5399 5.67716 10.729 5.86632C10.9182 6.05548 10.9241 6.35846 10.7468 6.55474L10.729 6.57342L6.14588 11.1566C5.95062 11.3518 5.63403 11.3518 5.43877 11.1566C5.24351 10.9613 5.24351 10.6447 5.43877 10.4495L10.0219 5.86632Z"></path></svg><span class="copied-indicator docsearch-exclude" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></span></span></a></div>
<p>Every spec generates three key files that form the foundation of your specification:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>requirements.md</strong> (or <strong>bugfix.md</strong>) - Captures user stories, acceptance criteria, or bug analysis in structured notation</li>
<li><strong>design.md</strong> - Documents technical architecture, sequence diagrams, and implementation considerations</li>
<li><strong>tasks.md</strong> - Provides a detailed implementation plan with discrete, trackable tasks</li>
</ul>
<!--$!--><template data-dgst="BAILOUT_TO_CLIENT_SIDE_RENDERING"></template><div class="my-8 flex justify-center rounded-lg bg-zinc-900/50 p-4">Loading diagram...</div><!--/$-->
<div class="heading-anchor-wrapper"><h2 id="three-phase-workflow">Three-Phase Workflow</h2><a href="#three-phase-workflow" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Copy link to Three phase workflow section"><span class="anchor-icon-wrapper"><svg class="anchor-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 17" width="16" height="17" fill="currentColor"><path d="M2.27143 14.228C0.591897 12.5485 0.591897 9.82539 2.27143 8.14585L3.42324 6.99404L3.44188 6.97635C3.63816 6.799 3.94118 6.80488 4.13035 6.99404C4.31951 7.18321 4.32539 7.48623 4.14804 7.6825L4.13035 7.70115L2.97854 8.85296C1.68953 10.142 1.68953 12.2319 2.97854 13.5209L3.00888 13.5509C4.30014 14.8098 6.36751 14.7998 7.64646 13.5209L8.79827 12.3691L8.81691 12.3514C9.01319 12.174 9.31621 12.1799 9.50538 12.3691C9.69454 12.5582 9.70046 12.8613 9.52311 13.0576L9.50538 13.0762L8.35356 14.228C6.68715 15.8944 3.99347 15.9074 2.311 14.267L2.27143 14.228ZM13.0215 3.47793C11.7425 2.19899 9.67517 2.18896 8.3839 3.44794L8.35356 3.47793L7.22579 4.6057C7.03053 4.80096 6.71395 4.80096 6.51869 4.6057C6.32342 4.41044 6.32342 4.09386 6.51869 3.89859L7.64646 2.77082L7.68603 2.73177C9.3685 1.09138 12.0622 1.10441 13.7286 2.77082L13.7677 2.81036C15.3952 4.47958 15.3951 7.14417 13.7677 8.81338L13.7286 8.85296L12.6008 9.98073C12.4056 10.176 12.089 10.176 11.8937 9.98073C11.6985 9.78547 11.6985 9.46888 11.8937 9.27362L13.0215 8.14585L13.0515 8.11551C14.3005 6.83442 14.3005 4.78937 13.0515 3.50827L13.0215 3.47793Z"></path><path d="M10.0219 5.86632L10.0406 5.84858C10.2369 5.67124 10.5399 5.67716 10.729 5.86632C10.9182 6.05548 10.9241 6.35846 10.7468 6.55474L10.729 6.57342L6.14588 11.1566C5.95062 11.3518 5.63403 11.3518 5.43877 11.1566C5.24351 10.9613 5.24351 10.6447 5.43877 10.4495L10.0219 5.86632Z"></path></svg><span class="copied-indicator docsearch-exclude" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></span></span></a></div>
<p>All specs follow a three-phase workflow that transforms your idea into executable implementation:</p>
<p><strong>Requirements or Bug Analysis</strong> - Define what needs to be built or fixed</p>
<ul>
<li>Feature Specs: User stories and acceptance criteria in <code>requirements.md</code></li>
<li>Bugfix Specs: Bug analysis with current/expected/unchanged behavior in <code>bugfix.md</code></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Design</strong> - Create technical architecture and implementation approach in <code>design.md</code></p>
<ul>
<li>System architecture and component design</li>
<li>Sequence diagrams and data flow</li>
<li>Error handling and testing strategy</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Tasks</strong> - Generate discrete, executable implementation tasks in <code>tasks.md</code></p>
<ul>
<li>Trackable tasks with clear outcomes</li>
<li>Real-time status updates as you implement</li>
<li>Run tasks individually or all at once</li>
</ul>
<div class="heading-anchor-wrapper"><h2 id="task-execution">Task Execution</h2><a href="#task-execution" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Copy link to Task execution section"><span class="anchor-icon-wrapper"><svg class="anchor-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 17" width="16" height="17" fill="currentColor"><path d="M2.27143 14.228C0.591897 12.5485 0.591897 9.82539 2.27143 8.14585L3.42324 6.99404L3.44188 6.97635C3.63816 6.799 3.94118 6.80488 4.13035 6.99404C4.31951 7.18321 4.32539 7.48623 4.14804 7.6825L4.13035 7.70115L2.97854 8.85296C1.68953 10.142 1.68953 12.2319 2.97854 13.5209L3.00888 13.5509C4.30014 14.8098 6.36751 14.7998 7.64646 13.5209L8.79827 12.3691L8.81691 12.3514C9.01319 12.174 9.31621 12.1799 9.50538 12.3691C9.69454 12.5582 9.70046 12.8613 9.52311 13.0576L9.50538 13.0762L8.35356 14.228C6.68715 15.8944 3.99347 15.9074 2.311 14.267L2.27143 14.228ZM13.0215 3.47793C11.7425 2.19899 9.67517 2.18896 8.3839 3.44794L8.35356 3.47793L7.22579 4.6057C7.03053 4.80096 6.71395 4.80096 6.51869 4.6057C6.32342 4.41044 6.32342 4.09386 6.51869 3.89859L7.64646 2.77082L7.68603 2.73177C9.3685 1.09138 12.0622 1.10441 13.7286 2.77082L13.7677 2.81036C15.3952 4.47958 15.3951 7.14417 13.7677 8.81338L13.7286 8.85296L12.6008 9.98073C12.4056 10.176 12.089 10.176 11.8937 9.98073C11.6985 9.78547 11.6985 9.46888 11.8937 9.27362L13.0215 8.14585L13.0515 8.11551C14.3005 6.83442 14.3005 4.78937 13.0515 3.50827L13.0215 3.47793Z"></path><path d="M10.0219 5.86632L10.0406 5.84858C10.2369 5.67124 10.5399 5.67716 10.729 5.86632C10.9182 6.05548 10.9241 6.35846 10.7468 6.55474L10.729 6.57342L6.14588 11.1566C5.95062 11.3518 5.63403 11.3518 5.43877 11.1566C5.24351 10.9613 5.24351 10.6447 5.43877 10.4495L10.0219 5.86632Z"></path></svg><span class="copied-indicator docsearch-exclude" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></span></span></a></div>

<div class="heading-anchor-wrapper"><h3 id="running-tasks-in-parallel">Running tasks in parallel</h3><a href="#running-tasks-in-parallel" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Copy link to Running tasks in parallel section"><span class="anchor-icon-wrapper"><svg class="anchor-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 17" width="16" height="17" fill="currentColor"><path d="M2.27143 14.228C0.591897 12.5485 0.591897 9.82539 2.27143 8.14585L3.42324 6.99404L3.44188 6.97635C3.63816 6.799 3.94118 6.80488 4.13035 6.99404C4.31951 7.18321 4.32539 7.48623 4.14804 7.6825L4.13035 7.70115L2.97854 8.85296C1.68953 10.142 1.68953 12.2319 2.97854 13.5209L3.00888 13.5509C4.30014 14.8098 6.36751 14.7998 7.64646 13.5209L8.79827 12.3691L8.81691 12.3514C9.01319 12.174 9.31621 12.1799 9.50538 12.3691C9.69454 12.5582 9.70046 12.8613 9.52311 13.0576L9.50538 13.0762L8.35356 14.228C6.68715 15.8944 3.99347 15.9074 2.311 14.267L2.27143 14.228ZM13.0215 3.47793C11.7425 2.19899 9.67517 2.18896 8.3839 3.44794L8.35356 3.47793L7.22579 4.6057C7.03053 4.80096 6.71395 4.80096 6.51869 4.6057C6.32342 4.41044 6.32342 4.09386 6.51869 3.89859L7.64646 2.77082L7.68603 2.73177C9.3685 1.09138 12.0622 1.10441 13.7286 2.77082L13.7677 2.81036C15.3952 4.47958 15.3951 7.14417 13.7677 8.81338L13.7286 8.85296L12.6008 9.98073C12.4056 10.176 12.089 10.176 11.8937 9.98073C11.6985 9.78547 11.6985 9.46888 11.8937 9.27362L13.0215 8.14585L13.0515 8.11551C14.3005 6.83442 14.3005 4.78937 13.0515 3.50827L13.0215 3.47793Z"></path><path d="M10.0219 5.86632L10.0406 5.84858C10.2369 5.67124 10.5399 5.67716 10.729 5.86632C10.9182 6.05548 10.9241 6.35846 10.7468 6.55474L10.729 6.57342L6.14588 11.1566C5.95062 11.3518 5.63403 11.3518 5.43877 11.1566C5.24351 10.9613 5.24351 10.6447 5.43877 10.4495L10.0219 5.86632Z"></path></svg><span class="copied-indicator docsearch-exclude" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></span></span></a></div>
<p>When you run all tasks on a spec, Kiro analyzes your task list, figures out which tasks depend on each other, and runs independent tasks concurrently. For most feature specs, this cuts execution time significantly without any setup.</p>
<p>Kiro builds a <strong>dependency graph</strong> of the tasks in your <code>tasks.md</code> and groups independent tasks into <strong>waves</strong>:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Wave 1</strong> - all tasks with no dependencies. These run concurrently.</li>
<li><strong>Wave 2</strong> - all tasks whose dependencies were satisfied by Wave 1. These run concurrently.</li>
<li><strong>Wave N</strong> - continues until all tasks are complete.</li>
</ul>
<p>Waves execute sequentially; tasks within a wave execute concurrently.</p>
<!-- -->
<div class="heading-anchor-wrapper"><h2 id="types-of-specs">Types of Specs</h2><a href="#types-of-specs" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Copy link to Types of specs section"><span class="anchor-icon-wrapper"><svg class="anchor-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 17" width="16" height="17" fill="currentColor"><path d="M2.27143 14.228C0.591897 12.5485 0.591897 9.82539 2.27143 8.14585L3.42324 6.99404L3.44188 6.97635C3.63816 6.799 3.94118 6.80488 4.13035 6.99404C4.31951 7.18321 4.32539 7.48623 4.14804 7.6825L4.13035 7.70115L2.97854 8.85296C1.68953 10.142 1.68953 12.2319 2.97854 13.5209L3.00888 13.5509C4.30014 14.8098 6.36751 14.7998 7.64646 13.5209L8.79827 12.3691L8.81691 12.3514C9.01319 12.174 9.31621 12.1799 9.50538 12.3691C9.69454 12.5582 9.70046 12.8613 9.52311 13.0576L9.50538 13.0762L8.35356 14.228C6.68715 15.8944 3.99347 15.9074 2.311 14.267L2.27143 14.228ZM13.0215 3.47793C11.7425 2.19899 9.67517 2.18896 8.3839 3.44794L8.35356 3.47793L7.22579 4.6057C7.03053 4.80096 6.71395 4.80096 6.51869 4.6057C6.32342 4.41044 6.32342 4.09386 6.51869 3.89859L7.64646 2.77082L7.68603 2.73177C9.3685 1.09138 12.0622 1.10441 13.7286 2.77082L13.7677 2.81036C15.3952 4.47958 15.3951 7.14417 13.7677 8.81338L13.7286 8.85296L12.6008 9.98073C12.4056 10.176 12.089 10.176 11.8937 9.98073C11.6985 9.78547 11.6985 9.46888 11.8937 9.27362L13.0215 8.14585L13.0515 8.11551C14.3005 6.83442 14.3005 4.78937 13.0515 3.50827L13.0215 3.47793Z"></path><path d="M10.0219 5.86632L10.0406 5.84858C10.2369 5.67124 10.5399 5.67716 10.729 5.86632C10.9182 6.05548 10.9241 6.35846 10.7468 6.55474L10.729 6.57342L6.14588 11.1566C5.95062 11.3518 5.63403 11.3518 5.43877 11.1566C5.24351 10.9613 5.24351 10.6447 5.43877 10.4495L10.0219 5.86632Z"></path></svg><span class="copied-indicator docsearch-exclude" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></span></span></a></div>
<p>Kiro supports two types of specs to match your development needs:</p>
<div class="heading-anchor-wrapper"><h3 id="feature-specs">Feature Specs</h3><a href="#feature-specs" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Copy link to Feature specs section"><span class="anchor-icon-wrapper"><svg class="anchor-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 17" width="16" height="17" fill="currentColor"><path d="M2.27143 14.228C0.591897 12.5485 0.591897 9.82539 2.27143 8.14585L3.42324 6.99404L3.44188 6.97635C3.63816 6.799 3.94118 6.80488 4.13035 6.99404C4.31951 7.18321 4.32539 7.48623 4.14804 7.6825L4.13035 7.70115L2.97854 8.85296C1.68953 10.142 1.68953 12.2319 2.97854 13.5209L3.00888 13.5509C4.30014 14.8098 6.36751 14.7998 7.64646 13.5209L8.79827 12.3691L8.81691 12.3514C9.01319 12.174 9.31621 12.1799 9.50538 12.3691C9.69454 12.5582 9.70046 12.8613 9.52311 13.0576L9.50538 13.0762L8.35356 14.228C6.68715 15.8944 3.99347 15.9074 2.311 14.267L2.27143 14.228ZM13.0215 3.47793C11.7425 2.19899 9.67517 2.18896 8.3839 3.44794L8.35356 3.47793L7.22579 4.6057C7.03053 4.80096 6.71395 4.80096 6.51869 4.6057C6.32342 4.41044 6.32342 4.09386 6.51869 3.89859L7.64646 2.77082L7.68603 2.73177C9.3685 1.09138 12.0622 1.10441 13.7286 2.77082L13.7677 2.81036C15.3952 4.47958 15.3951 7.14417 13.7677 8.81338L13.7286 8.85296L12.6008 9.98073C12.4056 10.176 12.089 10.176 11.8937 9.98073C11.6985 9.78547 11.6985 9.46888 11.8937 9.27362L13.0215 8.14585L13.0515 8.11551C14.3005 6.83442 14.3005 4.78937 13.0515 3.50827L13.0215 3.47793Z"></path><path d="M10.0219 5.86632L10.0406 5.84858C10.2369 5.67124 10.5399 5.67716 10.729 5.86632C10.9182 6.05548 10.9241 6.35846 10.7468 6.55474L10.729 6.57342L6.14588 11.1566C5.95062 11.3518 5.63403 11.3518 5.43877 11.1566C5.24351 10.9613 5.24351 10.6447 5.43877 10.4495L10.0219 5.86632Z"></path></svg><span class="copied-indicator docsearch-exclude" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></span></span></a></div>
<p>For building new features and capabilities in your application. Feature Specs guide you through requirements gathering, technical design, and implementation planning with two workflow variants: Requirements-First and Design-First. For well-understood features, you can also use <a class="" href="/docs/specs/quick-spec/">Quick Spec</a> to auto-generate all three artifacts without approval gates.</p>
<p><a class="" href="/docs/specs/feature-specs/">Learn more about Feature Specs →</a></p>
<div class="heading-anchor-wrapper"><h3 id="bugfix-specs">Bugfix Specs</h3><a href="#bugfix-specs" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Copy link to Bugfix specs section"><span class="anchor-icon-wrapper"><svg class="anchor-icon" viewBox="0 0 16 17" width="16" height="17" fill="currentColor"><path d="M2.27143 14.228C0.591897 12.5485 0.591897 9.82539 2.27143 8.14585L3.42324 6.99404L3.44188 6.97635C3.63816 6.799 3.94118 6.80488 4.13035 6.99404C4.31951 7.18321 4.32539 7.48623 4.14804 7.6825L4.13035 7.70115L2.97854 8.85296C1.68953 10.142 1.68953 12.2319 2.97854 13.5209L3.00888 13.5509C4.30014 14.8098 6.36751 14.7998 7.64646 13.5209L8.79827 12.3691L8.81691 12.3514C9.01319 12.174 9.31621 12.1799 9.50538 12.3691C9.69454 12.5582 9.70046 12.8613 9.52311 13.0576L9.50538 13.0762L8.35356 14.228C6.68715 15.8944 3.99347 15.9074 2.311 14.267L2.27143 14.228ZM13.0215 3.47793C11.7425 2.19899 9.67517 2.18896 8.3839 3.44794L8.35356 3.47793L7.22579 4.6057C7.03053 4.80096 6.71395 4.80096 6.51869 4.6057C6.32342 4.41044 6.32342 4.09386 6.51869 3.89859L7.64646 2.77082L7.68603 2.73177C9.3685 1.09138 12.0622 1.10441 13.7286 2.77082L13.7677 2.81036C15.3952 4.47958 15.3951 7.14417 13.7677 8.81338L13.7286 8.85296L12.6008 9.98073C12.4056 10.176 12.089 10.176 11.8937 9.98073C11.6985 9.78547 11.6985 9.46888 11.8937 9.27362L13.0215 8.14585L13.0515 8.11551C14.3005 6.83442 14.3005 4.78937 13.0515 3.50827L13.0215 3.47793Z"></path><path d="M10.0219 5.86632L10.0406 5.84858C10.2369 5.67124 10.5399 5.67716 10.729 5.86632C10.9182 6.05548 10.9241 6.35846 10.7468 6.55474L10.729 6.57342L6.14588 11.1566C5.95062 11.3518 5.63403 11.3518 5.43877 11.1566C5.24351 10.9613 5.24351 10.6447 5.43877 10.4495L10.0219 5.86632Z"></path></svg><span class="copied-indicator docsearch-exclude" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></span></span></a></div>
<p>For systematically diagnosing and fixing bugs with surgical precision while preventing regressions. Bugfix Specs help you identify root causes, design fixes, and validate that nothing else breaks.</p>
<p><a class="" href="/docs/specs/bugfix-specs/">Learn more about Bugfix Specs →</a></p>

focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 mt-2" style="animation-duration:0s"><ol>
<li>From the Kiro pane, click the <code>+</code> button under <strong>Specs</strong>. Alternatively, choose <strong>Spec</strong> from the chat pane.</li>
<li>Kiro will ask if you are developing a Feature or fixing a Bug<!-- -->
<ul>
<li>If you choose Feature, describe your feature and choose your workflow: <strong>Requirements-First</strong> or <strong>Design-First</strong></li>
<li>If you choose Bug, describe your bug</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Follow the workflow through each phase to implementation</li>
</ol><div class="relative"><video loop="" muted="" playsInline="" aria-label="Creating a specification in Kiro" class="rounded-lg border shadow-lg w-full" src="/videos/specs-start.mp4?h=4bef81b6" autoPlay=""></video><button class="shadow-subtle drop-shadow-subtle absolute bottom-2 right-2 z-50 flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-prey-700 bg-purple-300 p-2 text-prey-750 backdrop-blur-lg transition-colors hover:bg-purple-500 md:bottom-6 md:right-6 " aria-label="Play video" type="button"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M12.5409 6.77436C13.4022 7.3796 13.3883 8.67163 12.4992 9.25471L5.69763 13.7155C4.7001 14.3697 3.375 13.6541 3.375 12.4611L3.375 3.53962C3.375 2.34669 4.7001 1.63107 5.69763 2.28529L12.4992 6.74604L12.5409 6.77436ZM11.9508 8.41847C12.2471 8.2241 12.2518 7.79342 11.9647 7.59169L11.9508 7.58229L5.14923 3.12153C4.81672 2.90345 4.375 3.14198 4.375 3.53962L4.375 12.4611C4.375 12.8526 4.803 13.0898 5.13355 12.8892L5.14923 12.8792L11.9508 8.41847Z" fill="currentColor"></path></svg></button></div></div><div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_48stbsnpiv5aivb_-trigger-cli" hidden="" id="radix-_R_48stbsnpiv5aivb_-content-cli" tabindex="0" class="ring-offset-background focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 mt-2"></div><div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_48stbsnpiv5aivb_-trigger-web" hidden="" id="radix-_R_48stbsnpiv5aivb_-content-web" tabindex="0" class="ring-offset-background focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-offset-2 mt-2"></div></div>
